A worktree shares the id and a moved checkout keeps it; `projects/<slug>--<id8>/project.md` (OKF `project`) records the source and every root seen and is updated under the markdown store lock (`MarkdownMemoryStores.updateResourceFile`). `project.id` overrides detection; an invalid id or unresolvable root disables the project scope with one warning, and enabling no level fails validation. The scoped policy layer's `scopeRoot` now locates its ledger only and no longer has to equal the memory's `resourceId`.

- Memory lifecycle defect fixes (plan 188.1 Task 11 — D4, D5, D13):
  - D4: Superseded reflections retire their promoted notes with supersedes semantics (`validTo` set, correction lineage preserved, logged `superseded-by-om`). Replacement reflections created in the same pass are linked via `supersedes`. Verified notes edited after promotion (carrying correction lineage) are not retired, marked `reconsolidate: true`, and counted in `memory:status`.
  - D5: Verification by use, not retrieval: `memory.recall` drops unconditional `touch` and `recordUse`. Recalled and injected note IDs share a unified `FabricCitationTracker`. Usage increments only when the assistant cites a note (`[id]`) in turn completions, keyed by distinct session ID. The recall tool output prompts: `"Cite [id] for notes you use."`.
  - D13: Archive separate from validity: `archiveStale` sets `status: "archived"` and `archivedAt`, never setting `validTo`. Active recall, injection, and directory walks exclude archived notes via a unified `isActiveNote` predicate in `lifecycle.ts`. Historical time-scoped recall (`asOf` prior to `archivedAt`) sees archived, non-superseded notes as valid.

- Fabric OKF export and import for every store: `exportMemoryOkf(memory, { dir, stores })` writes the thread's notes, working memory, tombstones and holds, `index.md`, and `log.md` in the markdown layout (no embeddings), and `importMemoryOkf(memory, { dir, stores, mode })` reads a bundle into any store (SQLite, Postgres, in-memory, markdown). Imports stamp `generated.by: import`, scan every note and quarantine hits as candidates, re-embed only when an embedder is passed, and never clear or overwrite a target's legal hold. New `memory:export { dir }` and `memory:import { dir, mode }` commands resolve `dir` inside `tools.root` (plan 188.1 Task 7).

- Fabric provenance and poisoning defenses: every durable write stamps `generated: { by, at }` (`agent` with the session id, `promotion` with the reflection and evidence ids, `human` for an external edit found by reconcile, `import`, or `host`), and the markdown store's `log.md` records the actor on each line. Reconcile runs `scanScopedMemoryContent` once per externally changed file: a hit sets `status: candidate` with `flags: [<class>]`, rewrites the note, and logs `flagged`. A flagged note is never injected passively and `memory.recall` renders it `[flagged: <class>]`; only `memory.replace` or a clean human edit lifts it. Every injected fabric block (working, directory, semantic tail) is marked `untrusted`, `inert`, and `injectionCapable`. The scan is heuristic, so `log.md` and `git diff` are the audit path (MINJA).

- Fabric store defaults to the OKF markdown store: with `tools.root` set, durable notes and working memory persist as browsable files under `<tools.root>/notes` (canonical note files, derived `.index.sqlite`), selected with `store: "markdown" | { markdown: { dir } }`; `{ sqlite }` and `{ postgres }` stay available but need `memory:export` to be inspectable. The file reconcile and the directory walk skip `notes/**` as user files (the directory names `notes/index.md`), and a new `memory:reindex` command rebuilds the derived index and reports `indexed`/`removed`/`malformed` counts (`{ full: true }` re-reads every file). `memory.replace { id }` and `correct()` rewrite the note file in place: a `corrected` self-invalidation no longer archives it.

- Memory core `./fabric`: `createMarkdownMemoryStores({ root, index?, dimensions? })` stores each durable record as an OKF v0.2 markdown file under `<root>/<tenant>/<resource-path>/<dir>/` and keeps a derived, rebuildable SQLite index at `<root>/.index.sqlite`. Frontmatter carries the OKF fields plus a `prism:` map and round-trips exactly; embeddings never reach the files. Working memory is `<resource-path>/working/<thread>.md` and control state (invalidations, ACL, share grants, generations) is `<resource-path>/.state.json`. Writes are file-first under a cross-process `<root>/.lock`; `index.md`/`log.md` are rewritten per batch; `reconcile()` re-indexes changed files and `rebuild()` every file; a missing or corrupt index is rebuilt from files plus state. Paths are jailed with a `realpath` check, files are `0o600` and directories `0o700`, and `log.md` never contains note text.

- Fabric retrieval is lexical by default: `retrieval: "lexical"` (the new default) searches notes with the store's lexical leg and needs no embeddings package, while `retrieval: "hybrid"` adds the vector leg through the `embeddings` service and falls back to lexical when no service is present. `CreateMemoryOptions.embedder` is optional and `Memory.retrieval` reports the derived mode; without an embedder, writes carry `embedding: []` and recall runs through `lexicalQuery`. `RecallOptions.hybrid` is removed (the mode is fixed at creation). Consolidation, the linker/evolution workers, and the scoped activation floor now use the active mode's [0,1] similarity (term-set Dice in lexical mode, pre-fusion cosine in hybrid); the scoped read policy applies its floor to lexical coverage too. `memory.recall` accepts `queries` (up to 4 phrasings fused by RRF), renders ±200-character snippets with matched terms and note paths under a default 1,200-token budget, and hints when a result is empty or weak. `semantic: true` no longer requires an embeddings service.

- Memory core: one lexical analyzer (`lexicalTerms` with stopwords and conservative stemming, `lexicalCoverage`, `lexicalDice`, `lexicalKeys`) shared by every lexical leg. Stores accept embedding-less (lexical-only) records; every `lexicalQuery` ORs analyzed terms and reports `coverage`. The SQLite store moves to schema version 2 (BM25 over analyzed terms with note keys weighted above the body). Postgres allows a NULL embedding and fixes the `countByKind` collation error.

- Fabric background promotion from observational memory: opt-in `promote: { observational, verifyAfterUses, archiveAfterDays }` writes new reflections as `candidate` facts (`metadata.status`) through the normal write path after the trust scan. Candidates are never injected passively or counted in the directory, are marked `(candidate)` in `memory.recall`, become verified by use in distinct runs, agent confirmation, or `fabric.verify(id)`, and are archived when unused. Adds `Memory.patchMetadata`, `fabric.verify`/`recordUse`, and a `memory:status` command.

- Fabric memory directory: a capped, write-versioned `Long-term memory` prefix block (counts by kind, top topics, recent files, working-block labels; no content, no model or embedding call) so the agent knows what long-term memory holds when passive injection is off. `memory.view` with no path pages the full topic and file lists. New `directory` config (default on); `Memory.countByKind` / `Memory.topTags` and optional `VectorStore.countByKind` / `topTags` on the in-memory, SQLite, and Postgres stores.

- Fabric passive semantic injection is off by default. Opt-in injection uses fabric filtering, a pre-fusion cosine floor, hybrid retrieval, task queries, per-user-turn caching, deduplication, citation touches, and tail placement. Notes use augmented embedding keys; all stores support batched `Memory.get` for one-hop links outside the candidate batch.

- Fabric consolidation preserves similar notes with different text and exposes `similar` links in recall explanations. Explicit supersession retires the referenced note; an opt-in, bounded model decider supports ADD/UPDATE/SUPERSEDE/NOOP with safe fallback.

- Memory: neutral importance is 0.5; access-based recency and explicit batched `Memory.touch` are available. Core now owns `fuseRankings` (replacing RAG fusion exports) and `withTailContext` with accounting metadata. Postgres adds nullable `last_accessed_at`.


- Fabric tools accept typed fact/procedure inserts and exact in-place replacement of blocks, files, and note ids, with durable-content trust scanning and correction lineage.

- Memory fabric defaults to jailed SQLite durability under `tools.root`, persists Postgres working memory, and reconciles changed/deleted files on startup without re-embedding unchanged files.

- Observational memory keeps prompt prefixes stable between compactions: own summaries replace duplicate injection, foreign compactions pin a bounded ledger, and status reports the context mode. Own compaction follows advancing observation coverage; observer history is bounded to a quarter of the drop target.

- Observational memory grounds facts in source timestamps, renders dated observations and reflection ranges, supports explicit supersession, and keeps one bounded current task with citation guidance and context item metadata.

### Added

- Memory-core adds `./sqlite` with durable vector and working stores, exact cosine retrieval, FTS5 BM25, atomic version checks, and persisted scope, ACL, invalidation, generation, and share state. New database files use mode 0600.

- **Memory benchmark harness.** Added an opt-in pinned memory benchmark with full-context and observational-memory replay, wire usage accounting, injection logs, exact paired McNemar comparisons, and offline smoke checks. Renamed the scoped fixture probe to `probeRecallSmoke` and its input/report key to `recallSmoke`.

- **Provider conformance recordings replay and provider package scaffolding.** `providerConformance` (`@arnilo/prism/provider-kit`) gains a `recordings` option supporting directory paths and URLs. It replays recorded scenarios through `replayFetch` with signature tracking, asserting event order, usage accounting invariants (`cacheReadTokens + cacheWriteTokens <= inputTokens`), streaming tool-call reconstruction, terminal `done` stop reason, thinking replay signatures (failing unsigned thinking replay), abort pre-check, provider-owned header priority, and no secret leaks, returning a structured `ProviderConformanceResult`. `prism new package --provider <name>` scaffolds a modern standalone provider package with `createRoutedProvider`, catalog in `models()`, conformance test running `providerConformance({ recordings })`, `wire/` recordings directory, and credential-gated `live.test.ts`. `docs/provider-kit.md` and `docs/testing.md` document the conformance suite and generator.

- **OpenAI Chat codec options and routed-provider builder.** `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit` exports `createRoutedProvider` for unified multi-route provider construction (`routes: Record<string, RoutedProviderRoute>`) with fail-closed route resolution, pre-fetch requirement validation (`require.sessionId`), provider-owned header priority, single-pass credential resolution, bounded redaction of error bodies, and codec dispatch. `createOpenAICompatibleProvider` and the kernel's built-in provider are refactored onto `createRoutedProvider`. `openAIChatBody` and `openAIChatEvents` (`src/provider-kit/codecs/openai-chat.ts`) absorb codec options for `reasoningReplay` (`"reasoning_content" | "fold" | "drop"`), `imageInput` (`"capability-gated" | "always"`), `maxTokensField` (`"max_tokens" | "max_completion_tokens"`), and shared thinking/compat resolvers (`resolvePreserveThinking`, `resolveReasoningEffort`, `resolveThinkingObject`, `resolveReasoningObject`, `stripOwnedCompat`). `examples/custom-provider.ts` demonstrates an offline multi-route custom provider using `createRoutedProvider` and `replayFetch`. `docs/provider-kit.md` documents writing a provider package using the routed builder.

- **Cache planner defaults and affinity routing.** `PromptCacheBreakpointLocation` adds `"last_message"` for marking the newest message in a turn (`messages.length - 1`), while preserving `"last_stable_message"` for hosts requiring stable turn boundaries. `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit` exports `resolvePromptCachePlan` (target de-duplication before `maxBreakpoints` enforcement, definition-level caching when `tools` breakpoint is specified) and `anthropicCacheEnabled`. Codecs and `createRoutedProvider` support route affinity (`routes[x].affinity?: (request) => Record<string, string>`) for pinning prompt cache sessions to upstream backend nodes. `docs/models.md` and `docs/provider-kit.md` document prompt cache defaults and affinity.

- **`KERNEL_API_LEVEL` names the kernel API a package targets.** `@arnilo/prism` exports `KERNEL_API_LEVEL` (`1`), `src/version.ts` carries `SUPPORTED_API_LEVELS`, and `PackageRequires.api` is required while `PackageRequires.prism` becomes an optional minimum. The loader rejects a package whose level the kernel does not support with `PackageLoadError` `kernel_api`, and a definition with no `requires` with `definition_invalid`; every first-party `definePackage` declares `requires: { api: 1 }`. `docs/packages.md` documents the field.


- **Shared tier-1 live provider scenario suites.** `@arnilo/prism/testing` exports `runProviderLiveScenarios`, `runSystemOneLiveScenarios`, `executeProviderLiveScenarios`, `executeSystemOneLiveScenarios`, `checkLayaServerReachable`, and individual scenario runners (`liveScenarioTextStream`, `liveScenarioToolLoop`, `liveScenarioStructuredOutput`, `liveScenarioAbort`, `liveScenarioErrorResponse`, `liveScenarioCacheProbe`, `systemOneScenarioDecision`, `systemOneScenarioToolsRejected`, `systemOneScenarioAbort`, `systemOneScenarioErrorResponse`). All 4 first-party provider packages (`hyper`, `opencode-go`, `typesafe`, `laya`) run these standardized suites in their `live.test.ts`. Hermetic test `src/__tests__/provider-live-scenarios.test.ts` validates all scenarios over `replayFetch` offline.

- **Supported-provider gate and repository requirements.** `src/__tests__/packaging.test.ts` asserts that every provider package under `packages/providers/*` ships non-empty wire recordings in `src/__tests__/wire/`, a `live.test.ts` running `runProviderLiveScenarios` or `runSystemOneLiveScenarios`, a package test invoking `providerConformance` or `systemOneConformance`, and an active entry in `scripts/live-matrix.json`, tested via negative fixture testing (`provider_package_requires_recordings_live_suite_and_matrix_row`). `docs/provider-kit.md` and `docs/contributing.md` document repository shipping requirements for provider packages.

- **The safety-gate invariants ship as a conformance check.** `@arnilo/prism/testing/tool-conformance` gains `assertGateIntegrity({ createAgent })`, which runs four probes against the loop a factory builds — a rewritten call is re-gated, an `ask` keeps another handler's sanitizing rewrite, a resumed call is re-gated, and `dispose()` stops an in-flight tool — and throws naming the probe on the first violation, so a custom loop that skips the tool pipeline fails a published check instead of shipping silently. The mock provider is the only dependency. `docs/testing.md` documents it and `docs/loop.md` links it from the safety-gate rule.

- **Wire recorder and replay harness.** Added `scripts/wire-proxy.mjs`, a loopback-only Bun HTTP proxy that streams requests and responses unchanged, records exchanges to `<n>.json` (preserving method, path, request body, status, content-type, and raw response body while excluding authorization and transport headers), enforces mandatory secret scanning (`--secret-env`, exiting 3 on secret detection), and appends raw upstream usage across both OpenAI and Anthropic formats (`--usage-log`). `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit` exports `replayFetch` for ordered offline request matching and `assertRecordedStreamConforms` for protocol conformance verification against recorded wire fixtures. `docs/live-testing.md` and `docs/provider-kit.md` document the harness.

### Changed

- **The compat gate freezes declaration shapes per entry point.** `scripts/release.mjs gate` now records, for every name reachable from a package's `exports` target, the full normalized declaration: interface, class and enum bodies (members sorted, comments stripped), type alias right-hand sides, every overload, and re-exports resolved to their declarations. Lines are keyed `<entry> <name>`, so a root → subpath move shows as a removal and an addition. Before, the gate kept one name list per package that included internal declarations and missed plain `export interface` / `export type`. A new optional member, a new overload, or a new member of a kernel-produced union (`PRODUCER_ONLY_TYPES`) passes as `compatible`. Every other change fails with a line diff unless you pass `--allow-break` with a migration note. All live baselines were regenerated in the new format, and the orphan `@arnilo/prism-agent-sdk` and `@arnilo/prism-work` baselines were deleted. `docs/release.md` ("The compat gate") documents the rules.
- **TypeSafe Jev and Laya providers rebuilt with single shape on System One.** `@arnilo/prism-ai/typesafe` and `@arnilo/prism-ai/laya` are rebuilt over `systemOneProviderPackage` in `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit` with identical package structure (`index.ts`, `models.ts`, `provider.ts`). Standardized `*_DEFAULT_BASE_URL`, `*_DEFAULT_API_KEY_REF`, and `*_API_KEY_ENV` naming (`DEFAULT_LAYA_BASE_URL` and `layaBaseUrl` removed). Added plaintext remote endpoint warning in System One printing origin only. `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit` exports `systemOneConformance`, with offline wire fixtures and live skip probes added for both providers.
- **Hyper provider rebuilt on the routed builder with usage monetization and offline recordings.** `@arnilo/prism-ai/hyper` is rebuilt on `createRoutedProvider` with `openai` (`/chat/completions`) and `anthropic` (`/messages`) routes, auto-routing `qwen3.6-*` to messages and all other models to chat completions. Surfaced Hyper usage monetization (`usage.cost` and `usage.remaining`) directly into `Usage.cost`, `Usage.currency`, and `Usage.metadata.hyper`. Added offline wire recordings in `packages/providers/hyper/src/__tests__/wire/` (`openai` and `anthropic`) with full `providerConformance({ recordings })` coverage. Removed obsolete internal wire and retry helpers (`hyperChatBody`, `hyperChatEvents`, `serializeHyperChatMessage`, `classifyHyperError`, `hyperHttpError`, `applyHyperAnthropicCacheControl`, `hyperAnthropicCacheEnabled`, `hyperPreserveThinking`, `hyperReasoningEffort`, `hyperThinking`, `stripHyperOwnedCompat`).
- **OpenCode Go provider rebuilt on the routed builder with session correlation and affinity.** `@arnilo/prism-ai/opencode-go` is rebuilt on `createRoutedProvider` with `openai` (`/chat/completions`) and `anthropic` (`/messages`) routes, routing `minimax-*` and `qwen*` to messages and all other models to chat completions. Enforced pre-dispatch session correlation requirement (`require: { sessionId: true }`) and session affinity routing (`x-opencode-session` header derived from sanitized `cacheKey ?? sessionId`, clamped to 128 characters). Added offline wire recordings in `packages/providers/opencode-go/src/__tests__/wire/` (`openai` and `anthropic`) with full `providerConformance({ recordings })` coverage. Removed obsolete internal wire, cache, and thinking helpers (`openAIChatBody`, `openAIChatEvents`, `serializeOpenCodeGoChatMessage`, `anthropicMessagesBody`, `anthropicMessagesEvents`, `applyOpencodeAnthropicCacheControl`, `opencodeAnthropicCacheEnabled`, `opencodeOwnedHeaders`, `opencodeSessionId`, `openCodeGoPreserveThinking`, `openCodeGoReasoning`, `openCodeGoReasoningEffort`, `openCodeGoThinking`, `stripOpenCodeGoOwnedCompat`).
- **Prompt cache defaults mark newest message.** `DEFAULT_CACHE_BREAKPOINTS` is updated from `system_prompt` + `last_stable_message` to `[{ location: "system_prompt" }, { location: "last_message" }]`, reducing the uncached tail in agentic multi-turn loops to at most 1 turn. The `tools` breakpoint now marks the **last tool definition** on codecs supporting definition-level caching (Anthropic Messages `tools` array) rather than a tool message; `resolveBreakpoint` returns `-1` for `tools`. `applyCacheControl` safely inspects content blocks in reverse (up to 20 blocks) to prevent cache markers from landing on empty or whitespace-only content blocks or empty tool results.
- **First-party peer ranges admit the whole 0.x kernel line.** Every `peerDependencies["@arnilo/prism"]` is `>=0.13.0 <1.0.0` instead of `^0.13.0`, and `scripts/release.mjs` accepts the window in both validate modes, so a kernel 0.14 no longer forces a package release. `roadmap.md` Part 3, `docs/packages.md`, and `docs/migrate-to-0.13.md` document the API-level rule.
- **Manifest `integrity` covers the package's full runtime dependency closure.** `install` records and `verifyInstalled` validates `integrity` as the canonical SRI `sha512` hash over the installed package and every package in its runtime dependency closure (dependencies and non-optional `peerDependencies`, resolved with Node resolution within the managed project, each hashed with `hashPackageTree` and combined in sorted name order). A changed byte in any package, a missing dependency (`package_missing`), a symlink anywhere in the closure (`integrity_mismatch`), or a closure over `MAX_CLOSURE_PACKAGES` (512) fails closed before any import. `docs/manifest.md` and the manifest JSON Schema document it.
- **First-party packages read their version from their own `package.json`.** Every `definePackage` call, the ACP `agentInfo`, and the MCP server identity (`serverVersion` default and `new McpServer`) now use `manifest.version` instead of the retired `0.12.1` literal, so a bump is one edit and `agent.inspect()` can never report a version the published manifest does not. The MCP client identity (`new Client`) and the MCP Apps `hostInfo` read the manifest too. `scripts/version-literal-gate.test.mjs` fails on a reintroduced literal — a string-literal `name` that is not a workspace package is an in-process test/conformance package and stays allowed.
- **Templates and the e2e coverage ledger name only 0.13 packages.** The catalog manifests, `templates/README.md`, and the `deep-research`/`business-worker` scaffold bodies use `@arnilo/prism-provider-*`, `@arnilo/prism-tools/web`, `@arnilo/prism-memory-rag`, and `@arnilo/prism-tools/work`. `scripts/e2e-coverage.json` is regenerated for the grouped workspace set (retired family keys dropped, 64 dangling suite paths pruned; four surfaces stay pending in baseline mode). A new `templates_load` test checks every catalog manifest's `packages` against the live workspace set and rejects a retired name or a raw secret value. The provider-kit retired-name scan allows the committed `scripts/deprecations-0.13.json` ledger, which must name every retired package.
- **Unified inclusive `Usage` contract across provider codecs.** `Usage.inputTokens` represents total prompt tokens across all providers (inclusive of cached tokens); `cacheReadTokens` and `cacheWriteTokens` are non-negative subsets (`cacheReadTokens + cacheWriteTokens <= inputTokens`). `Usage.totalTokens` is derived as `inputTokens + outputTokens` when an upstream vendor omits it. `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit` exports `normalizeUsage`, which all codecs use: Anthropic Messages normalizes wire `input_tokens` to inclusive `input_tokens + cache_read_input_tokens + cache_creation_input_tokens`, while OpenAI Chat Completions maps inclusive `prompt_tokens`. `cacheHitRate` is bounded in `[0, 1]`, `assertUsageAccounting` enforces `cacheReadTokens + cacheWriteTokens <= inputTokens`, and `CostCatalog` pricing separates uncached input tokens from cached tokens to prevent double-charging. `docs/models.md` and `docs/provider-kit.md` document the contract.

### Fixed

- Observational memory reflections now replace prior reflections and consume their input observations while retaining recall evidence. Budget drops preserve reflections; worker tools declare argument schemas, rendering keeps newest observations, and the dropper runs independently with a validated 20,000-token default.

- **Anthropic Messages codec wire correction against real streams.** Relocated Anthropic Messages codec from `src/provider-kit/anthropic-messages.ts` to `src/provider-kit/codecs/anthropic-messages.ts` (re-exported via `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit`). The codec now ignores `event: ping` keep-alive frames, field-merges `message_start` and `message_delta` usage into normalized inclusive usage, yields `providerUsage` with `message_delta.stop_reason`, captures `signature_delta` into thinking blocks, parses `redacted_thinking` content blocks (`ThinkingContent.redacted`, `ThinkingContent.data`), replays signed thinking with signatures and unsigned thinking as text when `preserveThinking: true`, maps upstream stream `error` events to `providerError` (classifying `overloaded_error` and `api_error` as transient), and emits terminal `providerError` on malformed frames or JSON parse failures. Offline recorded wire suites are added for both `@arnilo/prism-ai/hyper` and `@arnilo/prism-ai/opencode-go` covering text, tool loop, thinking, caching, and stream errors.
- **A clean frozen-lockfile install works again.** Every workspace declared the kernel (`@arnilo/prism`) as `file:../../..` while the kernel is the workspace root; Bun re-resolved that self-reference on every install, so `bun install` rewrote `bun.lock` and `bun ci` could never freeze it. The root now links itself (`"@arnilo/prism": "file:."` in its `devDependencies`), the per-package `file:` deps and their `postinstall` symlink are gone, and `bun.lock` is regenerated with integrity hashes. `scripts/install-idempotence.test.mjs` runs in `release:gate` and fails if a plain install rewrites the lock. `docs/contributing.md` documents the install.
- **The SQLite store opens under a concurrent open.** `@arnilo/prism-extensions/store-sqlite` runs `PRAGMA busy_timeout` before `foreign_keys`/`journal_mode`, so two connections opening the same file at once no longer race the journal-mode lock into `SQLiteError: database is locked` (the loser waits up to the configured timeout instead of failing); `sqlite-persistence.test.ts` pins the pragma order.
- **The headless scheduler reads its clock once per loop iteration.** `@arnilo/prism-headless` used one reading to decide which schedules are due and a second to decide how long to wait, so a clock that advanced between the two dropped the due tick: the loop parked on an unrelated wake until something else changed a schedule. One reading now drives both decisions, and `scheduler.test.ts` pins a jump between the old two readings.
- **The `tool.call` gate now sees the arguments that execute.** `RunController.executeTools` evaluates `tool.call` in up to two rounds: a `rewrite` becomes the call's arguments and the gate runs again on them, so a later handler's `deny` (including `@arnilo/prism-tools/coding`'s hard deny) holds under any rewrite and an `ask` no longer drops a sanitizing rewrite. Conflicting round-1 rewrites deny with `rewrite_conflict`, a round-2 rewrite that changes the arguments again denies with `rewrite_unstable`, the payload `args` is a deep-frozen copy, and `tool.execute` is wrap-only (`HookError` `ERR_PRISM_HOOK_EXECUTE_REWRITE`). `@arnilo/prism-extensions/hooks-json` replays its `PreToolUse` decision per call so its handler runs once; the driver-kit `RunController.ask()` primitive applies the same rule. `docs/hooks.md` and `docs/tools.md` document it.
- **A resumed call runs the full tool pipeline again.** `session.resume()` replays every approved pending call through `RunController.executeTools`, so `tool.call` (two rounds), `tool.execute`, `tool.result`, and the transcript append run exactly as for a fresh turn — a `deny` on the final arguments (including a `modifiedArguments` rewrite or a later `rewrite`) holds and the tool never executes. The decision that answered an `ask` satisfies it again only for the exact call it approved (tool name and arguments); a call a rewrite moved off those arguments is staged as a new pending decision and the run suspends instead of prompting a host that already answered. `reject_*` decisions still settle as a blocked `tool.completed`. `docs/agent.md` and `docs/hooks.md` document it.
- **Ownership scopes narrow only.** `SessionOptions.ownership` scopes a session inside the agent's declared `AgentConfig.ownership`, and `RunOptions.ownership` narrows the session's effective scope; a field that changes an enclosing value throws `OwnershipScopeError` (`code: "ERR_PRISM_OWNERSHIP_WIDEN"`) before any event, where `RunOptions.ownership` previously replaced the agent scope and could widen it. Every entry a session writes carries the effective scope, opening an existing session checks the whole branch against the session's scope, and every run and resume re-checks the branch against the run's scope before the first write — a branch with any entry outside it is `SessionNotFoundError`. `agent.sessions.list/get/delete/search` share the one `narrowScope` helper and keep their query-filter behavior (the agent's scope wins without throwing). `docs/agent.md` and `docs/host-security.md` document it.
- **`agent.dispose()` fails closed.** It now aborts every active run with reason `agent_disposed`, closes every open session (settling background tasks within `limits.backgroundSettleMs`), and only then removes packages and hooks; it is idempotent and a second call awaits the first. After it starts, `agent.session()` and `agent.commands.run()` throw `AgentDisposedError` (`code: "ERR_PRISM_AGENT_DISPOSED"`), an active run ends as `run.failed` with `error.code: "agent_disposed"`, and `RunController.executeTools` denies every call for a loop that ignores its abort signal, so no tool executes after `dispose()` begins. Previously `dispose()` unwound packages while runs and sessions stayed live. `docs/agent.md` documents the order.
- **`@arnilo/prism-remote` partitions sessions, HostUi routing, pending-decision delivery, and agent events by principal.** `ServerState.sessions` is keyed by `principalKeyOf(principal)` and session id with an $O(1)$ `sessionOwners` index, so cross-principal calls to `sessions.open(id)`, `sessions.messages`, `run`, `steer`, `abort`, `sessions.resume`, and `commands.run` reject with `session_not_found`. `ServeAgentOptions.ownershipFor` threads a host-derived `OwnershipScope` into `sessions.open`, `sessions.list` (stripping client-supplied ownership fields), `run`, `sessions.resume`, and reconnect `deliverPending` resume; `ServeAgentOptions.operator` restricts agent-scoped (`sessionId`-less) `remoteHostUi` requests and `agent.event` notifications to operator connections while session-scoped requests and events reach only the session's owner. Single-principal transports keep their existing behavior. `packages/hosts/remote/README.md` and `docs/host-security.md` document it.
- **`@arnilo/prism-remote` allow-lists wire decisions, steer options, session list queries, and parent links.** `packages/hosts/remote/src/wire.ts` validates `sessions.resume` decisions (`approvalId` ≤ 256 chars, valid `ApprovalOutcome`, `reason` ≤ 2 KiB, `elicitation` JSON object only for pending elicitation decisions, and `modifiedArguments` rejected with `invalid_params` unless `ServeAgentOptions.allowModifiedArguments: true`), `steer` options (`softInterrupt: boolean` only), `sessions.list` queries (documented `SessionListQuery` filter/pagination keys with bounded `limit`/`cursor` and client ownership keys stripped), and `sessions.open.parent` (`SessionParentLink` keys ≤ `MAX_SESSION_LINK_ID_LENGTH`), removing unvalidated wire casts in `serve.ts`.
- **`@arnilo/prism-ag-ui` enforces narrow-only session ownership on open, run, and resume.** `createAgUiHandler` now awaits `agent.session({ id: threadId, create, ownership: authorization.ownership })` before opening the SSE response stream and passes the same `ownership` scope on `session.stream` and `session.resume`. `SessionNotFoundError` and `OwnershipScopeError` map to `404 ERR_PRISM_AG_UI_NOT_FOUND` (`Session not found`) prior to streaming so absent and foreign threads are indistinguishable, and the in-memory `pending` interrupt map records `authorization.ownership` so resuming from another scope rejects with `400 ERR_PRISM_AG_UI_INPUT`. `packages/hosts/ag-ui/README.md` documents the behavior and thread-id squatting guidance.
- **`@arnilo/prism-devtools` rejects non-loopback Host and Origin headers by default.** Every route validates `Host` against allowed hosts (`127.0.0.1:<port>`, `localhost:<port>`, `[::1]:<port>` by default) and `Origin` against allowed origins, answering `403 ERR_PRISM_DEVTOOLS_FORBIDDEN_HOST` or `403 ERR_PRISM_DEVTOOLS_FORBIDDEN_ORIGIN` to block DNS-rebinding attacks on loopback inspectors. `ServeDevtoolsOptions` (`DevtoolsOptions`) adds `allowedHosts` and `allowedOrigins` to override the defaults. `packages/hosts/devtools/README.md` documents it.
- **Obscura URL validation resolves target hostnames before spawning.** `validateObscuraWebUrl` in `@arnilo/prism-tools/web` now resolves target hostnames via the kernel's `defaultResolver` (with support for custom `resolver` in `ObscuraWebToolsOptions`) and rejects private, loopback, link-local, and cloud metadata addresses through `assertSsrfAllowedUrl` on each address candidate (`ERR_OBSCURA_SSRF`). `packages/tools/web/README.md` documents the pre-check, the single resolution cost, the time-of-check gap, and the contained-proxy recommendation.
- **`@arnilo/prism-tools/coding` warns when shell runs unsandboxed on the host.** When `sandbox` is `"none"` and the `shell` tool is registered, setup emits `status.note("warn", "shell runs on the host without a sandbox; pair it with @arnilo/prism-extensions/approval or set sandbox")`. `packages/tools/coding/README.md` gains an "Enterprise deployment" section covering sandbox backends, `@arnilo/prism-extensions/approval`, `commandRules`, and `egress` policies.
- **Pinned fetch proves the `lookup` hook is honored under Bun.** `src/__tests__/pinned-fetch.test.ts` adds `pinned_lookup_is_honored_for_unresolvable_host`, verifying that `requestPinned` against an unresolvable `.invalid` hostname connects to a local loopback listener via the pinned address.
- **Provider factories split factory-only `fetch` from JSON config.** Provider factories accept `fetch` for provider-factory uniformity and do not route it into JSON package config. The `provider_factories_split_fetch` test walks every provider package and asserts the split.
- **The prefix-stability fixture fails loudly instead of reporting "0 captured requests".** `runPrefixStabilityConformance` and the test's capture fixture assert each run's `status` is `completed` and name the run's own error, so a failed run can no longer pass as an empty capture. The fixture stopped registering a second `load_skill` (progressive disclosure already registers the kernel's built-in; the duplicate failed both runs) and dropped the dead `activeSkills` run option — `limits.maxToolRounds` is the only run option the kernel reads.
- **The durable-event conformance probe pages with a limit any source accepts.** `assertAgentEventSourceConforms` (`@arnilo/prism/testing`) requested `limit: 10`, which a source configured with a smaller `maxPageSize` rejects by contract, so the kernel's own probe failed against the kernel's own reference source (`createMemoryAgentEventSource`, and any database adapter configured the same way). The probe now walks the tail one record at a time, which is page-size agnostic and exercises cursors harder. `src/__tests__/agent-event-source-conformance.test.ts` runs the probe against the reference source and fails if the probe accepts a source that drops cursors.
### Removed

- **Vendor-specific provider-kit modules and OpenAI Responses helpers removed from `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit`.** Deleted `openai-responses`, `openai-uploads`, `openai-cache`, `openai-thinking`, `openai-compat`, `oauth`, `oauth-device-code`, `image-generation`, and `model-discovery`. `createOpenAIResponsesProvider`, `pollDeviceCodeToken`, `refreshOAuthCredential`, `revokeOAuthCredential`, and related vendor-specific symbols are removed; custom providers should use vendor-neutral primitives (`createOpenAICompatibleProvider`, `createAnthropicMessagesProvider`, `createSystemOneProvider`, etc.). Hyper's `responses` route is removed; requesting `compat.route: "responses"` rejects fail-closed with `ProviderRequirementError { requirement: "route" }`.
- **17 provider packages removed.** 0.13.0 ships four provider packages: `@arnilo/prism-provider-{hyper, opencode-go, typesafe, laya}`. The 17 other provider packages (`@arnilo/prism-provider-{ai-sdk, alibaba, anthropic, azure, bedrock, clinepass, commandcode, deepseek, google, kimi, neuralwatt, ollama, openai, openrouter, vertex, xai, zai}`) are removed; custom or third-party providers can be built on `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit`.
- **The 0.12 command shape is gone.** `CommandDefinition`, `CommandExecutionContext`, `CommandDrivers` (which carried the domain-specific `startWorkflow`), `CommandWorkflowRun`, and `CommandResult` are deleted with no alias. `PackageCommand` / `PackageCommandContext` are the only command contract: `run(args, { session, host, agent })` with `args` validated against the command's JSON schema. The wiki commands and the scoped-memory health command build `PackageCommand` directly (the `asPackageCommand` adapter is deleted), the ingest command's host-driver auto-filing path is dropped (nothing supplied drivers in 0.13, so it was dead), and `docs/migrate-to-0.13.md` carries the replacement row. `scripts/compat-baseline/arnilo__prism-memory-wiki.txt` records one removal and four changed signatures.

### Deprecated

- **The 0.13 split left 16 exports with no reader.** `scripts/dead-export-verify.mjs` classifies them in `docs/_evidence/dead-export-verification-2026-09-03.md` (14 deprecate, 2 remove); each carries `@deprecated` JSDoc naming "no replacement", runtime behavior is unchanged. Per package — `@arnilo/prism`: `applyNestedRun`, `suspendNested`, `MANIFEST_COMMANDS`; `@arnilo/prism-extensions/context-engineering`: `AttentionCompilerSetting`, `formatFoldedToolResult`, `isToolDisclosureError`, `resolveToolsDisclosure`; `@arnilo/prism-extensions/workflows`: `exactOwnershipKey`; `@arnilo/prism-extensions/compaction`: `HISTORY_SUMMARIZATION_PROMPT`, `UPDATE_SUMMARIZATION_PROMPT`, `ProviderRequestTransform` (marked for removal); `@arnilo/prism-memory-system/core`: `invalidateAcrossLayers`; `@arnilo/prism-extensions/governance`: `GovernanceEvaluation` (marked for removal), `governancePackage`; `@arnilo/prism-extensions/store-codecs`: `DEFAULT_MAX_HOLD_REASON_BYTES`; `@arnilo/prism-tools/web`: `WebToolSet`. They are removed at the next breaking cut.

## [0.13.0] - 2026-11-11

### Added

- **Kernel constraint gates.** `src/__tests__/kernel-constraints.test.ts` holds three gates: `kernel_has_no_runtime_dependencies` (no `dependencies`, `peerDependencies`, `optionalDependencies`, or `bundleDependencies`), `kernel_imports_only_builtins` (every `src/` specifier is relative, `node:*`, `bun`, or `bun:*`), and `kernel_imports_no_computed_specifier` (no specifier built at runtime). `scripts/budgets.json` gains a `kernel` row with five strict ceilings — packed bytes, unpacked bytes, file count, root exports, and non-test `src/` lines — enforced by `runGates` from the root tarball the tarball deny list already packs.
- **Frozen 0.13 surface.** `provider-kit`, `driver-kit`, `manifest`, and `testing` each carry their own frozen-export assertion beside the root freeze, and the driver-kit contract, the `RunController` driver primitives (`observe`, `ask`, `steers`), the `terminal` host request kind, the `prism relay` bin, and the closed redaction surface are each asserted by name.
- **Private reference host `apps/prism-agent`**, with a boundary gate that keeps its imports on the public package surface, a README walkthrough (agents, workflow graph, external and remote workers, `HostUi` routing, scheduling, entry points, and the offline scenarios) linked from `docs/index.md`, and a scenario suite that runs offline in `release:gate` after the client-neutrality check.
- **`@arnilo/prism-tools/research`**: research skills, a per-session source ledger, a read-only `cite` tool, and a bounded `run.end` citation check. `research.verify` returns the verdict.
- **Headless scheduler (`@arnilo/prism-headless`).** `createScheduler` for cron, one-off, and manual agent/workflow-command firings, with scoped CAS state, durable parent sessions, bounded concurrency, suspension recording, and cancellable deadline timers. Per-firing leases carry renewal and fencing metadata, restart missed policies are `skip` and `run-once`, and a live-worker once-only claim retains the documented at-most-once crash limitation. `routeHostUi` adds ordered routing, policy defaults, timeouts, and a value-free answer log.
- **`resumeWhenAnswered`** resumes routed approvals and elicitations after durable suspension, with abortable offers, scoped session authority, and shared coordination with `serveAgent`.
- **`@arnilo/prism-channels`: `channelHostUi`** lets a paired Telegram or Signal chat answer another agent's HostUi requests with authenticated one-use replies and value-free input correlation.
- **Remote agents (`@arnilo/prism-remote`).** WebSocket and HTTP/SSE remote-agent transports with bearer or host authentication, principal propagation, Host/Origin gates, and bounded streams; `@arnilo/prism-remote/testing` transport conformance for run, stream, steer, abort, resume, HostUi, commands, and disconnect suspension. The offline stdio remote-agent workflow example demonstrates local scripted approval, SQLite mirroring/search, and attribution documentation.
- **Workflow orchestration (`@arnilo/prism-extensions/workflows`).** Workflows run as a parent session and emit `workflow.node.started|completed|failed`, `workflow.gate`, `workflow.suspended`, and `workflow.notice` package events, persisted on the parent session. Node kinds: `agentNode` (any `Agent`, `parent` links, optional `worktree`), `gateNode`, `humanGate`, and `fanOut` (per-item routing; an item the driver kit refuses with `profile_busy` waits instead of failing). `startWorkflow(...)` returns a `WorkflowRun` whose `stream()` merges every child session's events with their `origin`, with `steer(nodeId, input)` and `abort()`. `resumeWorkflow` claims the run through the `leases` service and applies a per-node `idempotent` policy after a process kill. `workflowCommand`, `workflowLoop`, and `loopAgentNode` add command and single-agent loop entry points, and `workflowLoopStrategy` exposes the strategy for `loopConformance`. New examples: `examples/build-workflow.ts` and `examples/workflow-loop.ts`.
- **Origin-aware observability (`@arnilo/prism-extensions/observability`).** Spans carry `prism.origin.{agent,vendor,profile,executed_by,delegation_id,subagent_id}` (`ORIGIN_ATTRIBUTES`, `originAttributes`) when the event has `origin`; workflow instrumentation parents child `invoke_agent` spans on their node span; timeline steps carry `origin` and `ExecutionTimeline.byOrigin` groups them by agent then sub-agent, marking `executedBy: "external"` as observed.
- **Offline `examples/headless-host.ts`** demonstrates routed durable approval and a scheduled workflow on one SQLite file.

### Changed

- **Bun runtime floor is now `>=1.4.4`.** Every manifest declares `engines.bun >=1.4.4`, and CI, `packageManager` and `mise.toml` pin Bun 1.4.4. Bun 1.4.2 kept the old contents when `bun add file:<tarball>` replaced an installed package, so `prism add` recorded stale integrity for an updated local package.
- **Docs describe the 0.13 contract.** The 15 kernel pages formerly staged under `docs/next/` (agent, agent-adapters, drivers, events, hooks, host, loop, manifest, models, packages, provider-kit, release, slots, testing, tools) are promoted into `docs/`, replacing the 0.12 `hooks`, `testing`, and `tools` pages. `docs/index.md` is rewritten around the kernel pages, every package and host-library README, and the Prism Agent walkthrough. The 0.12 pages the promoted pages and the package READMEs replace are deleted, 19 pages with no 0.13 counterpart moved to `docs/history/`, and the `!docs/next` pack exclusion is removed.
- **The kernel publishes TypeScript source.** Every `exports` target is the module itself (`./src/index.ts`, `./src/testing/index.ts`, `./src/provider-kit/index.ts`, `./src/driver-kit/index.ts`, `./src/manifest/index.ts`) plus the emitted `./manifest/schema.json`, `bin.prism` runs `./src/cli.ts`, and `files` ships `src` minus `src/**/__tests__`. `dist/` is gone; `build:core` is a `tsc --noEmit` typecheck. The tarball is smaller (packed 1718164 → 1604691, unpacked 5584594 → 5113118, 646 → 468 files).
- **Provider wire helpers moved to `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit`.** `thinking`, `cache-helpers`, `provider-request-policy`, and `structured-output` leave the `@arnilo/prism` root and are exported only from the subpath. No root alias.
- **Package-owned kernel modules moved.** The event multiplexer and checkpoint-restore runner to `@arnilo/prism-extensions/workflows` (`CheckpointRestoreAudit` stays on the kernel); feedback preparation and the persistence-schema contract to `@arnilo/prism-extensions/store-codecs`; `describeStorage` to `@arnilo/prism-extensions/governance`; device policy to `@arnilo/prism-tools/computer-use`; citation integrity checks to `@arnilo/prism-evals`. Artifact record types and `citationBindingDigest` stay on the kernel.
- **`@arnilo/prism-extensions/delegate` reports each child through `origin`** instead of a bare `child`: `delegate.step` carries `{ origin, phase, turn?, status?, totalTokens? }` with `{ kind: "prism", agent, agentId, delegationId, depth: 1 }`, and `@arnilo/prism-extensions/observability`'s `handleDelegation` takes that `origin` and attributes the `prism.agent.delegate` span with the shared origin attributes. The 0.12 supervisor that produced `child` is gone, so `DelegationTelemetry.childId` and the `prism.delegation.child` attribute are removed with no alias.

### Fixed

- **Managed installs no longer share files with Bun's global cache.** `prism add`, `prism remove` and their rollback run Bun with `--backend=copyfile`. With Bun's default hardlinks, a tampered managed file also changed the cached package, and every later install of that tarball inherited the change.
- **`@arnilo/prism-hosts/driver-process` fails a run on an unhandled vendor request even when the binary exits first.** The JSON-RPC transport recorded `vendor_request_unhandled` only after writing the error reply; a binary that exited before the reply ended the run as a success.
- **The release gates cover every publishable workspace.** `release:gate` names the set from the root `workspaces` globs instead of a hand list, adds the `@arnilo/prism-driver-process` suite, runs all three reference adapters through `examples/adapters/conformance.ts` (now exiting non-zero on a failed leg), and runs the vendor-neutrality and kernel-constraint tests before `release.mjs gate`. `pack:dry-run` uses `bun pm pack --dry-run`, and the `npm ls` packaging guard is a Bun-native check: every declared dependency resolves from its workspace and no `node_modules/@arnilo` link dangles.
- **PostgreSQL `checkpoints` `keyPrefix` listing** returned an empty page on any database whose default collation is a locale (en_US.utf8 in the CI image): the `prefix + U+FFFF` range bound assumes code-point order, and that collation treats U+FFFF as ignorable. The bounds now compare `COLLATE "C"`. The durable `test:postgres` legs run and pass, so the K4 (`session-links`) and K5 (`external-state`) `storeConformance` legs plus `package-state` are recorded on Postgres rather than skipped, and release evidence records `pass` for that surface.
- **`@arnilo/prism-headless`:** the routed answer log breaks millisecond ties with a stored sequence, so offers recorded in the same millisecond (a channel timeout and the policy fallback) keep their offer order.
- **Fixes found by `examples/build-workflow.ts`.** A child run that ends `failed` now fails its `agentNode` with the child's error code (it used to pass an empty output), which is how a driver's `profile_busy` reaches `fanOut`'s pacing; a run refused at admission releases its worktree. `createWorkspaceService({ maxWorktrees })` is now honored by the git layer (the cap was silently 4 because the main tree counts against it).

### Removed

- **`@arnilo/prism-core` left the tree.** A2A left `@arnilo/prism-ag-ui` (no 0.13 catalog row). Trajectory schema checks use the kernel `validateJsonSchema`. Subpaths with no 0.13 home (server, supervisor, realtime, NATS sessions, OIDC, prompts, enterprise postgres) are deleted.
- **The three 0.12 workspaces `@arnilo/prism-coding-tools`, `@arnilo/prism-agent-sdk`, and `@arnilo/prism-acp-agent` left the tree**, along with the `packages/prism-providers` residue. The `/impeccable` persona pack is deleted (outside the 0.13 domain packs); the coding tool surface lives in `@arnilo/prism-tools/coding`, `@arnilo/prism-tools/openapi`, and `@arnilo/prism-tools/computer-use`; `@arnilo/prism-acp`'s `serveAcp` replaces the standalone ACP bin and fails closed on any client-supplied MCP server list.
- **The kernel checkpoint, lease, and checkpoint-backed run-state surface.** `CheckpointStore`, `LeaseStore`, their record/key/query types, `createMemoryCheckpointStore`, `createMemoryLeaseStore`, `CheckpointConflictError`, `LeaseConflictError`, `AgentConfig.runState`, `RunOptions.runState`, `AgentRunStateOptions`, `createAgentRunLifecycle`, `resumeAgentRun`, and `resumeAgentRunStream` are deleted with no alias. Durable runs anchor on the store slot's `suspended_run` entry and resume through `session.resume(decisions)`, which requires a decision for every pending request. `runCheckpointRestoreHooks` stays in the kernel.
- **The kernel workflow engine and its observability surface.** `defineWorkflow` and the graph engine moved to `@arnilo/prism-extensions/workflows`; the workflow spans, execution timeline, summaries, and RAG telemetry moved to `@arnilo/prism-extensions/observability`. `@arnilo/prism-core`'s `./governance/observability` and `./runtime/workflows` subpaths are deleted. A strict-subset approval batch now fails closed with `ERR_PRISM_DECISION_INVALID` instead of re-suspending with the remainder, and a nested-run decision fails closed with `ERR_PRISM_DECISION_INVALID` until a delegation carrier exists.
- **The kernel prompt runner** (`prism -p`, `--provider`, `--model`, and the rest of the 0.12 agent flags) and `cli-providers`. The `prism` bin is the manifest CLI plus `prism relay`.
- **The legacy kernel RPC server** (`runRpcServer`, `parseRpcRequest`) and `prism --mode rpc`; use `@arnilo/prism-remote` with `serveAgent` and `connectAgent`.
- **Dead kernel modules:** run bundle, use-case model selection, cache telemetry, provider capture, the memory persistence-lifecycle factory, conversation helpers, agent-definition resolution, and settings providers. Lifecycle types moved to `@arnilo/prism-extensions/store-codecs`, and `createMemoryAgentEventSource` moved to `@arnilo/prism/testing`. `isTerminalAgentEventType` and `AgentEventSourceError` stay.
- **Kernel speech, transcription, batch, moderation, video, and realtime contracts.** Speech, transcription, batch, moderation, and realtime now export from `@arnilo/prism-provider-openai`. Video exports from `@arnilo/prism-provider-alibaba`. Image generation exports from `@arnilo/prism/provider-kit` (openai and alibaba both implement it; a sibling import is forbidden). `embeddings` stays, and `ModelCapabilities` no longer carries those modality flags.
- **Subpaths** `./node/config`, `./node/settings`, `./node/agent-definitions`, `./testing/agent-event-source-conformance`, `./testing/persistence-schema`, and `./testing/feedback`.
- **`packages/prism-code`** removed from the repository; `@arnilo/prism-code` 0.4.1 stays on npm until the deprecation.

## [0.12.1] - 2026-09-29

> **Twelve publishable packages** (plus the independently versioned `@arnilo/prism-agent-sdk` and `@arnilo/prism-code`). Lockstep patch from 0.12.0. It exists because the published `@arnilo/prism-code@0.4.0` declares `@arnilo/prism@^0.12.0` and imports `capToolResultSummary`, a new root export: without 0.12.1 a fresh `bun add -g @arnilo/prism-code` resolves the published 0.12.0 and fails to load. No breaking changes; every other delta below is additive.

### Added
- **Prism Code release pipeline (plan 140 Task 5).** Pushing `@arnilo/prism-code@<version>` runs the independent release verification/publish for that package (already-published lockstep packages are skipped via `--resume`, and the SDK publishes before Prism Code in topological order), then creates the tag-derived `prism-code-v<version>` GitHub Release from the six archives and `SHA256SUMS` with the package `CHANGELOG.md` section as notes; a pre-release version creates a draft instead. `release.yml`'s `post-publish-smoke` job installs `@arnilo/prism-code@<version>` from the registry and runs the published `install.sh` against that release, which must print `<version> (binary)`.
- **Prism Code native curl installer (plan 140 Task 4).** `install.sh` installs the standalone binary with no Bun or npm: it detects OS/arch/libc (glibc or musl), resolves the version from `--version`/`PRISM_CODE_VERSION` or the npm registry, downloads the release archive and `SHA256SUMS` over HTTPS, verifies the SHA-256, and installs atomically to `${PRISM_HOME:-$HOME/.prism}/bin` (binary `0755`, directories `0700`). It prints the exact bash/zsh/fish PATH line, edits the rc only with `--modify-path`, upgrades in place, and `--uninstall` removes the binary without touching user data. `scripts/install-sh.test.mjs` exercises it against a local HTTPS fixture server, plus an Alpine musl container leg.
- **Session titles and faithful resume replay (`@arnilo/prism-code`, `@arnilo/prism`, `@arnilo/prism-core`).** Sessions carry a first-prompt title (`SESSION_TITLE_METADATA_KEY`, ≤ 80 redacted chars) via an atomic `mergeSessionMetadata` write, `/rename <title>` replaces it, and `SessionSearchHit` carries a `title` plus `messageCount`. CLI/TUI resume gains `--session`, `--continue`, and `--resume`; resumed history replays persisted entries through the live TUI reducer (real tool success/error/denied status, collapsed results, thinking blocks, compaction summaries, last-run model restore).
- **Resilient MCP plane (`@arnilo/prism-agent-sdk`).** `assembleMcpPlane` connects servers in parallel with a per-server `connectTimeoutMs` (default 15 s, `DEFAULT_MCP_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`), reports per-server `connected`/`failed`/`disabled` status, enforces the host `mcp.allow` list, and exposes `reconnect(serverId)`/`close()`. HTTP specs accept `headers` and OAuth `auth`; status errors are redacted. `AgentSdkDefinition.mcp` exposes the live status and controls, and `McpConnectError` wraps runtime failures.
- **`ToolRegistry.unregister?(name)`** optional method so host registries can drop re-bridged MCP tools.
- **Prism Code MCP servers (`@arnilo/prism-code`).** Per-server `headers` values may embed `${env:NAME}`/`${credential:NAME}` references resolved at connect time, `auth: "oauth"` persists tokens in the credential store under `mcp:<serverId>` (`createMcpAuthState`/`createMcpAuthOptions`), and `connectTimeoutMs`/`enabled` are supported; URL servers default `allow` to the URL origin. Project-declared servers are gated by a per-repo fingerprinted trust prompt recorded in `~/.prism/trust.json` (0600), with `--trust-project-mcp` as the headless/ACP escape hatch.
- **Host policy stubs on MCP specs (`@arnilo/prism-agent-sdk`).** `disabledReason` and `preflightError` are reported as `disabled`/`failed` status without allow validation or connection.
- **`/mcp` command and live MCP footer (`@arnilo/prism-code`).** Bare `/mcp` lists every configured server with state, tool count, transport, and redacted error/reason from the cached plane status; `/mcp tools <id>` lists bridged tools; `/mcp reconnect <id>` re-bridges in the background without blocking input; `/mcp login <id>` runs the OAuth round trip for an `auth: "oauth"` URL server (opens the authorization URL, then accepts the pasted callback URL or code); `/mcp disable|enable <id>` unregisters or restores a server's tools for the session. The status footer now renders `MCP: connected/total` with failed servers highlighted, and `AgentSdkMcpPlane` exposes `getServerTools(serverId)` for hosts.
- **Prism Code standalone binaries (plan 140 Task 3).** `scripts/build-prism-code-binaries.mjs` compiles `@arnilo/prism-code` with `bun build --compile` into deterministic, self-tested executables for `linux-x64`, `linux-arm64`, `linux-x64-musl`, `linux-arm64-musl`, `darwin-x64`, and `darwin-arm64`. They embed the OpenTUI native library, the keyring addon, and every provider adapter, and need no Bun on the host. `.github/workflows/prism-code-binaries.yml` builds each target on a native runner, self-tests it, archives it as `prism-code-<target>.tar.gz`, and writes and attests `SHA256SUMS`. See [docs/release-and-install.md](docs/release-and-install.md#prism-code-standalone-binaries).

### Changed
- **`prism-code --version` reports the install channel** for support diagnostics: `0.4.0 (bun)` for the package install, `0.4.0 (binary)` for the standalone executable.
- **Prism Code and the agent SDK take their own version lines (plan 140).** `@arnilo/prism-code` is `0.4.0` and `@arnilo/prism-agent-sdk` is `0.1.0`; neither shipped under the lockstep `0.12.0` number. **Breaking for `@arnilo/prism-code@0.3.x` users:** 0.4.0+ is the terminal app, and the former coding-agent profile library lives in `@arnilo/prism-coding-tools` and `@arnilo/prism-agent-sdk`. The app lists `@arnilo/prism`, `@arnilo/prism-providers`, and `@arnilo/prism-hooks` as regular dependencies (previously peers) so a global `bun add -g` install is self-sufficient, and it pins the SDK at `^0.1.0`. The SDK's `@arnilo/prism-coding-tools`, `@arnilo/prism-hooks`, and `@arnilo/prism-extensions/mcp` peers are now required, because its root entry imports all three statically.
- **`assembleMcpPlane` no longer throws on MCP connection failure.** A failing or hanging server is reported through `status` and never aborts `defineAgent` startup; invalid allow specs, duplicate `serverId`s, malformed host `mcp.allow` entries, and non-positive timeouts still throw `AgentSdkConfigError` before any process/network activity. Hosts that relied on the old throw-on-connect-failure contract should inspect `AgentSdkDefinition.mcp.status` (or catch `reconnect` failures as `McpConnectError`).
- **`formatHistoricalEntries` removed from `@arnilo/prism-code`.** Use `entriesToUiEntries` / `sessionEntryToUiActions` (`packages/prism-code/src/tui/history.ts`), which feed the same reducer as live events.
- **Compat baselines regenerated** on 2026-09-28 with `bun scripts/release.mjs gate --update-baseline` (twice: after Tasks 3–5, then again for Task 6's `formatFooterDetails` widening to accept `{ connected, total, failed }`), covering the removed export plus the additive session/MCP declarations above (`@arnilo/prism` +3, prism-core +14, coding-tools +29, prism-work +24 from pre-existing plan 136/137 surfaces, agent-sdk/prism-code baselines new). `gate` re-runs clean; regenerate again if later plan-138 tasks change surfaces.
- **Prism Code MCP config parsing is strict.** Unknown server keys, `command` + `url` conflicts, invalid transports, non-positive timeouts, and mis-placed fields fail closed instead of being silently dropped; literal secret headers (`Authorization`, `Cookie`, `X-API-Key`, …) are accepted only in the global `~/.prism/config.json`.
- **`mergeAgentConfig` keeps the full MCP plane config.** `allow`, `connectTimeoutMs`, `executionPolicy`, and `disabledReason`-carrying server lists previously survived the merge only partially (only `servers` and `connector` were carried), which silently dropped the host approval policy for bridged MCP tools; non-`servers` fields now merge with override precedence.

### Fixed
- **Bundled CLIs no longer hijack an app that bundles them (`@arnilo/prism-memory`, `@arnilo/prism-coding-tools`).** `prism-wiki` (`/wiki/cli`) and `prism-dev` (`/dev/cli`) now detect a direct run with `import.meta.main`. They used to compare `import.meta.url` with `process.argv[1]`, which is always true inside a `bun build --compile` binary, so a compiled Prism Code ran the wiki CLI instead of itself.

## [0.12.0] - 2026-09-26

> **Twelve publishable packages.** Lockstep minor from 0.11.1. Runtime is Bun `>=1.4.2`. Optional local document extraction is not a default install. See [docs/history/migrate-to-0.12.0.md](docs/history/migrate-to-0.12.0.md).

### Added
- **`@arnilo/prism-providers/decisions` typed decision call.** `askSystemOneDecisions(body, { timeoutMs?, maxStateBytes? }, signal?)` makes one `/v1/systemone` round trip and returns `{ model, answers, usage, timingMs }` with raw choice/score probabilities and confidence preserved; pre-fetch bounds (1–256 questions, 2–255 choice options, 2–10 score levels, 256 KiB state), typed auth/invalid-request/retry-exhausted/aborted errors, and deadline/abort composition into the existing retry loop. The `/typesafe` and `/laya` provider adapters still render schema text only. See [docs/providers/decisions.md](docs/providers/decisions.md).
- **Per-iteration replay forking with state and input injection.** `replayWorkflow` accepts `iteration?: number`, `injectState?: JsonObject`, and `injectInput?: unknown` in `ReplayWorkflowInput`. Targeted replay verifies the iteration exists in node execution history, restores checkpoint state at `stateVersionBefore` in $O(1)$ from `stateHistory`, merges injected state overrides, and seeds subsequent supersteps with deterministic lineage metadata. See [docs/workflows.md](docs/workflows.md).
- **Workflow state scoping & subgraph isolation (`withNodeScope`, `createScopedState`, `scopedSubgraphNode`).** Higher-order node wrapper `withNodeScope(scopeKey, node)` isolates node reads and updates to a sub-namespace within workflow state (`ctx.state = rootState[scopeKey]`), while exposing `ctx.rootState` for explicit cross-scope reads and validating scope keys against identifier traversal. `scopedSubgraphNode` executes nested workflows in isolated state namespaces with bidirectional input/output mapping. See [docs/workflows.md](docs/workflows.md).
- **Declarative event-driven swarm topology (`swarmRouterNode`, `defineSwarmWorkflow`).** Topic-based pub/sub multi-agent routing over cyclic supersteps. Agents publish events via `publishSwarmEvent(ctx, event)` and query triggering events via `getActiveSwarmEvents(ctx)`. `swarmRouterNode` matches topics (including wildcard patterns `topic:*` and `*`) against declared subscriptions and dynamically fans out to subscriber agents, draining cleanly to completion when all topic queues are empty. See [docs/workflows.md](docs/workflows.md) and [docs/multi-agent-patterns.md](docs/multi-agent-patterns.md).
- **Cyclic workflow graphs and dynamic routing (`routeNode`).** Wave-based superstep execution engine for cyclic workflows (`limits.maxSupersteps`, hard cap 256), node activation semantics (`activation: "all" | "any"`), dynamic branch selection via `routeNode({ select })` with fail-closed target validation against declared edges (`WorkflowRouteTargetError`, `ERR_PRISM_WORKFLOW_ROUTE_TARGET`), idle-drain completion, fail-closed host-unforgeable budget enforcement (`WorkflowSuperstepLimitError`, `ERR_PRISM_WORKFLOW_SUPERSTEP_LIMIT`), checkpoint schemaVersion 2 (`execution.superstep`, `execution.pending`, `execution.maxSupersteps`) with crash-consistent wave resumes, and cyclic replay resolution to latest succeeded iteration evidence. See [docs/workflows.md](docs/workflows.md).
- **Field-evidence guardrail (`createFieldEvidenceGuardrail`).** Opt-in `tool_input` guardrail beside claim grounding: each host-selected argument dot-path must resolve to a claim envelope (`{ value, source, path, revision? }`) that synchronous host-supplied evidence confirms for the same source and revision. Failures block with reason `field_evidence` and a violation token, never echo claimed or evidence values, and cap required fields (256) and evidence records (4,096). See [docs/guardrails.md](docs/guardrails.md).
- **Optional local document extraction.** `@arnilo/prism-work/document-extraction` converts supported bytes to Markdown with optional `@firecrawl/anydoc@0.2.4`. Scanned PDFs and explicit image OCR need a host-provisioned Docling worker (`docling/ocr.py`). Default hosts install neither. One OCR job. No upload by default.
- **`advertiseRecall` for observational-memory compaction.** `renderObservationalMemory` / `buildObservationalMemoryContextBlocks` can withhold the recall guidance sentence when the host exposes no recall tool; default `true` keeps existing output. See [docs/compaction-observational-memory.md](docs/compaction-observational-memory.md).

### Changed
- **Lockstep `0.11.1` → `0.12.0`.** All twelve manifests and internal caret ranges move together. The `src/index.ts` version constant is `"0.12.0"`.
- **SQLite is `bun:sqlite`.** The `better-sqlite3` peer is removed. Blob reads are `Uint8Array`. `lastInsertRowid` is a number unless `safeIntegers`. An `undefined` bind stores NULL. A `get()` miss is `null`. A Node import fails with the Bun runtime message.
- **Scaffolds emit Bun.** `prism init` writes `packageManager` `bun@1.4.2` and Bun install commands.
- **Coverage-safe observational-memory compaction.** The strategy keeps every eligible message entry after `latestObservationCoverageId`, not only the recent-message window, and leaves the fold boundary behind an unscanned prefix so a covered prefix can still compact. Automatic compaction defers when the observer skipped and no coverage cursor exists yet (`run_active`, `passive`, `missing_model`, `missing_credentials`, `in_flight`, `error`) instead of appending a per-turn compaction entry that folds nothing; `ObservationalMemoryFlushResult.skipped` is now the exported `ObservationalMemoryFlushSkipReason` union. See [docs/compaction-observational-memory.md](docs/compaction-observational-memory.md).

### Security
- The field-evidence verifier fails closed and keeps claimed and evidence values out of guardrail decisions. The typed decision call keeps timeout/error distinguishable from measured uncertainty and grants no effect authority; calibration stays host configuration.
- Route targets in `routeNode` are strictly constrained fail-closed to declared workflow edges (`targets ⊆ declaredSuccessors`), preventing arbitrary node injection or traversal of undeclared paths. Superstep execution budgets (`limits.maxSupersteps`) are host-unforgeable across crash restarts and resumes.

### Removed
- **Node runtime support (retired in 0.12.0).** All twelve publishable manifests declare `engines.bun >=1.4.2` and no longer declare `engines.node`; the two measured Node CI legs (the Node 24 public-import smoke and the `node22-compat` job) retired with them, and every install page moved to `bun add`. `engines.bun` is advisory metadata on Bun 1.4.2 — neither npm nor Bun enforces it — so the compensating control is the fail-closed import message plan 126 adds when the durable storage layer moves to `bun:sqlite`; the registry keeps the 0.11.x line for Node hosts, and `npm pack`/`npm publish`/`npm sbom` stay on the release host (plan 125 Task 5). See [docs/history/retire-node-runtime.md](docs/history/retire-node-runtime.md).

## [0.11.1] - 2026-09-24 (System One decision models)

### Added
- **`@arnilo/prism-providers/typesafe` and `@arnilo/prism-providers/laya`.** Structured-output decision models on `POST /v1/systemone`. Jev is hosted (`https://api.typesafe.ai`, env `TYPESAFE_API_KEY`). Laya is self-hosted `laya-serve` (default `http://localhost:8000`, optional `LAYA_API_KEY`). A request without `options.structuredOutput`, or with tools, fails before any network call. See [docs/providers/typesafe.md](docs/providers/typesafe.md) and [docs/providers/laya.md](docs/providers/laya.md).

## [0.11.0] - 2026-09-24 (store bounds, lease fence reset, subpath removals)

> **Twelve publishable packages.** Lockstep minor. No new public exports from the review remediation. Two host-visible store contracts change, and three subpaths that the published 0.10.0 tarball still shipped are removed. Predecessor published line is **0.10.0**. Registry/tag writes stay operator-authorized.

### Changed
- **Lockstep `0.10.0` → `0.11.0`.** All twelve publishable manifests move together with `^0.11.0` internal ranges. The lockfile, the `src/index.ts` version constant, the docs index banner, the release-workflow tag list, and the generated package-truth artifact agree (`scripts/version-literal-gate.test.mjs`).
- **Compat baseline regenerated.** The only declaration this cut changed is `version` (`"0.10.0"` → `"0.11.0"`). The same regen records the already-landed persona/graft removals (58 `@arnilo/prism-memory`, 48 `@arnilo/prism-coding-tools`) and the signature widenings already named in [docs/migration.md](docs/migration.md). No plan-120 public name was added.
- **Memory session store implements `readBranchPath`.** A snapshot walks the branch once and clones each kept entry once. JSONL and the durable adapters already had the method.
- **JSONL parse cache.** Reads after an in-process append reuse the parsed file when `(size, mtimeMs)` match. A same-size write inside one filesystem timestamp tick can still look unchanged; the upgrade path is hashing the file.
- **Idempotency window is 4,096 keys** on the memory store and the JSONL store. A replay of a key still inside the window is rejected. A replay of an evicted key appends a new entry. See [docs/migrate-to-0.11.md](docs/migrate-to-0.11.md).
- **In-memory lease sweep.** Expired rows are deleted once the map reaches 1,024. A released key that is still in the map inherits `fencingToken + 1`. A key that was swept starts its next fence at 1 (`docs/operations.md`). SQLite and Postgres adapters still keep the counter on the expired row.
- **Plain-text token estimates share one helper** (`ceil(length/4)`). Message and entry estimates are unchanged and are not billing numbers. Golden vectors live in `scripts/token-estimate-fixtures.json` (not shipped).
- **`npm test` gains two stages:** examples execution (spawns examples not already executed; `docker-process-session.ts` is `known-broken:placeholder-image`) and a Node branch-coverage audit (floor 83.49, `dist/**` only). Bun's coverage gate still records `branches: null`. Offline test budget pin is `< 110s`.
- **Freeze-test package presence** comes from `workspacePackageCounts()` in `scripts/package-truth.mjs`. Frozen count deltas stay in each suite.

### Removed
- **Behavior persona subpaths (`@arnilo/prism-coding-tools/caveman`, `/ponytail`).** Both subpaths, their vendored upstream fixtures, and the `@dietrichgebert/ponytail` optional peer are gone. Load the upstream tree with `loadSkillDirectory` and register skills from a host extension. `@arnilo/prism-coding-tools/impeccable` is unchanged. See [docs/migrate-to-0.11.md](docs/migrate-to-0.11.md).
- **Graft bridge subpath (`@arnilo/prism-memory/graft`).** The subpath, its `@nanonets/graft` optional peer, the fixture CLI, and the graft commands are gone. Integrate graft as a host MCP server, or author tools against `registerTool` / `registerCommand`. `/rag`, `/compaction/*`, `/fabric`, `/wiki`, and `/scoped` stay.

### Security
- No new credential surface. The lease sweep does not delete a live lease. Idempotency eviction cannot be used to skip the in-window duplicate check.

## [0.10.0] - 2026-09-21 (hook lifecycle completion, scoped agent memory)

> **Twelve publishable packages.** All 105–106 features ship behind their documented options and defaults: `@arnilo/prism-hooks` is the new twelfth package, and scoped memory is an opt-in subpath that does nothing until a host constructs it. Predecessor published line is **0.9.0**. Plan 097 (trajectory export) was not implemented for this line and is deferred rather than held unstarted against the cut. Registry/tag writes stay operator-authorized.

### Added
- **Scoped persistent memory (`@arnilo/prism-memory/scoped`).** New opt-in subpath: `createScopedMemoryPolicy({ memory, fabric, scopeRoot, policy })` is a policy and lifecycle layer over stores the host already configured (no fifth store). Create is inert and fails closed unless `scope.resourceId` equals the resolved `scopeRoot` and a `threadId` is set; the read path oversamples `fabric.recall`, applies an abstain floor (`activation.minSimilarity` 0.35) and activation budget (`topK` 3), ranks by `fabricScore × exp(−ageDays/tauDays) × (1 + ln(1 + uses))` (`tauDays` 30), and returns `{ hits: [], abstained: true }` rather than a weak hit. Writes go through a conservative post-run reviewer (`reviewSession`, strict JSON, garbage → zero writes), land as `candidate` records, and flip to `verified` after `promotion.reuseThreshold` (2) successful recalls — ledger status only, no fabric rewrite. `gcPass()` proposes archives onto `pending()` and never deletes (`legal_hold` skipped), `rememberFact` keeps a bounded working block (`maxChars` 2200, overflow throws `MemoryLimitError` with `consolidate first`), an injection scan plus an optional staged approval gate (`approval.default: "off"`) guard content, and a deterministic git audit mirror renders notes. State lives in one JSON ledger at `<scopeRoot>/.memory/state.json` (ids, counters, timestamps — never note content). Sizing: one reviewer call per run, one ledger read plus one write per recall, no model calls on the read path. See [docs/scoped-memory.md](docs/scoped-memory.md).
- **Scoped memory evaluation and health.** `runScopedMemoryEval` runs a win-rate A/B (with/without scoped memory) over a host-provided task set, `probePrecisionAt3` and `probeLocomoRecall` score the read path, `scopedMemoryHealth` reports counts plus conversion/activation/duplication rates, and `createScopedMemoryHealthCommand` exposes the same numbers as a command. See [docs/scoped-memory.md](docs/scoped-memory.md).
- **Stop hooks with bounded continuation.** `AgentConfig.stopHooks` / `RunOptions.stopHooks` run at the natural end of an agent loop with a `StopHookContext` (session and run ids, turn, redacted history, `stopHookActive`) and answer `{ action: "stop" }` or `{ action: "continue", reason }`; a continuation re-enters the loop with the reason queued as the next turn's steer — run-start input is never replayed — and `RunLimits.maxStopContinuations` (default 3, `0` disables, `null` uncaps) ends the run cleanly with the new `hook_limit` finish reason, which `checkpointPolicy: "every-turn"` leaves resumable. Extensions register hooks with `ExtensionAPI.registerStopHook()`. See [docs/hooks.md](docs/hooks.md).
- **Agent-event bridge and session lifecycle hooks.** `forwardAgentEvents(source, events, options?)` keeps the extension bus in step with a run (`agent_started` → `before_agent_start`, `turn_started` / `turn_finished` → `turn`, `tool_execution_started` → `tool_call`, `tool_execution_finished` → `tool_result`), delivering in event order with the original event as the read-only payload and never rethrowing into the run; core emits `session_start` once per session (durable resumes included) and `session_shutdown` on the new idempotent `AgentSession.close()`. See [docs/extensions.md](docs/extensions.md), [docs/middleware-hooks.md](docs/middleware-hooks.md).
- **Pre-compaction rewrite seam.** The `compaction_request` middleware hook runs after `compaction_started` and before the strategy, so a handler returns the `CompactionContext` the strategy actually compacts (entries and budget) and the following `compaction` hook plus `compaction_finished` event report that result. See [docs/middleware-hooks.md](docs/middleware-hooks.md).
- **`@arnilo/prism-hooks`, the hooks-file adapter.** New twelfth publishable package: `parseHooksConfig(text | object)` accepts the Claude flat and Codex `{ "hooks": { … } }` shapes, and `createHooksExtension(config, options)` compiles them onto public seams — `SessionStart` / `UserPromptSubmit` `additionalContext` through an instruction injector, `UserPromptSubmit` / `PreToolUse` / `PostToolUse` guardrails for denies, `tool_call` / `tool_result` middleware for `updatedInput` and post-tool context, and `Stop` onto stop hooks. Handlers are `command` (spawned shell-free, hash-pinned with `hookCommandHash()`) or `mcp_tool` (host-provided client, `${field.path}` templates), with seconds-based `timeout`, per-handler `additionalContextLimit` (default 2500 tokens, spilling to `<tempdir>/hook_outputs/`), and Codex's `continue: false` read as the stop signal. See [docs/hooks.md](docs/hooks.md).
- **Unified hook map.** [docs/hooks.md](docs/hooks.md) is the owning page for the hook model: the five families (transform, decide, inject, run-end control, observe), one row per Claude Code / Codex event mapped to its Prism surface or a documented non-goal with rationale (`PermissionRequest` stays host policy), the adapter reference, and migration steps from an existing `hooks.json` config.
- **Host skill-directory loader.** `loadSkillDirectory(directory, { maxSkillBytes? })` on `@arnilo/prism/node/contribution-discovery` loads a host-supplied `<dir>/<name>/SKILL.md` tree into inert `Skill[]`: one level, sorted, symlink-contained, per-file cap 262 144 bytes by default. It replaces the bounded reader the deleted persona subpaths carried. See [docs/contribution-discovery.md](docs/contribution-discovery.md).
- **Examples.** `examples/scoped-memory.ts` (scope guard → facts overflow → reviewer → abstain/hit recall → promotion → GC approval → mirror), `examples/hooks-json.ts` (a declarative `hooks.json` compiled onto guardrails, injectors, and stop hooks), and `examples/hooks-audit-command.mjs` (an audit-trail command handler).

### Changed
- **Lockstep `0.9.0` → `0.10.0`.** All twelve publishable manifests move together with `^0.10.0` internal ranges; the lockfile, the `src/index.ts` version constant, the docs index banner, the release-workflow tag lists, and the generated package-truth artifact agree (enforced by `scripts/version-literal-gate.test.mjs`).
- **Compat baselines regenerated at 0.10.0**: `+47` public names with **zero removals**, zero renames, and zero signature breaks (`@arnilo/prism` +6, `@arnilo/prism-memory` +41, `@arnilo/prism-hooks` new at 34). The only consumer-visible type addition is `AgentSession.close()`; see [docs/migration.md](docs/migration.md).
- **Budgets rebaselined with recorded reasons**: export ceilings `@arnilo/prism` 1456, `@arnilo/prism-memory` 934, `@arnilo/prism-hooks` 34, plus the non-null assertion ratchet; the root artifact diet is unchanged this cut.
- **Coverage thresholds gained the new package's row** (`@arnilo/prism-hooks` 84.7 / 78.67 / 90), so the coverage summary fails closed on a new package instead of passing silently.
- **Migration notes for 0.9.0 hosts**: [docs/migration.md](docs/migration.md) — the `AgentSession.close()` addition, `session_start` / `session_shutdown` now being emitted, the new `hook_limit` finish reason, the `maxStopContinuations` default, and the opt-in scoped-memory subpath.

### Removed
